=== BUGGY CODE ===
class Solution:
    def findAllRecipes(self, recepies: List[str], ingredients: List[List[str]], supplies: List[str]) -> List[str]:
        dct=defaultdict(lambda :[])
        indegree={}
        n=len(recepies)

        for i in recepies:
            indegree[i]=0

        for i in range(n+1)   
            for j in ingredients[i]:
                indegree[j]=0

        for i in range(n):
            for j in ingredients[i]:
                dct[j].append(recepies[i])
                indegree[recepies[i]]+=1

        st=[]
        for i in indegree:
            if indegree[i]==0:
                st.push(i)
        flst=[]
        ans=defaultdict(lambda :[])
        while st:
            x=st.pop(0)
            for i in dct[x]:
                for j in ans[x]:
                    if j not in ans[i]:
                        ans[i].append(j)
                ans[i].append(x)
                indegree[i]-=1
                if indegree[i]==0:
                    st.append(i)
            if x in recepies:
                for k in ans[x]:
                    if k not in supplies:
                        break
                else:
                    flst.append(x)
                    supplies.append(x)

        return iflst

class Solution:
    def findAllRecipes(self, recepies: List[str], ingredients: List[List[str]], supplies: List[str]) -> List[str]:
        dct=defaultdict(lambda :[])
        indegree={}
        n=len(recepies)

        for i in recepies:
            indegree[i]=0

        for i in range(n+1)   
            for j in ingredients[i]:
                indegree[j]=0

        for i in range(n):
            for j in ingredients[i]:
                dct[j].append(recepies[i])
                indegree[recepies[i]]+=1

        st=[]
        for i in indegree:
            if indegree[i]==0:
                st.push(i)
        flst=[]
        ans=defaultdict(lambda :[])
        while st:
            x=st.pop(0)
            for i in dct[x]:
                for j in ans[x]:
                    if j not in ans[i]:
                        ans[i].append(j)
                ans[i].append(x)
                indegree[i]-=1
                if indegree[i]==0:
                    st.append(i)
            if x in recepies:
                for k in ans[x]:
                    if k not in supplies:
                        break
                else:
                    flst.append(x)
                    supplies.append(x)

        return iflst

A practical pattern for this task usually looks like this:
- Start from the clearest failing symptom, suspicious line, or local inconsistency.
- Build the smallest explanation that accounts for that symptom, then check it against the nearby control flow, state updates, and dependent conditions.
- Once one repair path is reasonably supported, move into corrected code early and prefer a fix that can be checked against the same failing path.
- After the edit, rerun that path or the most relevant edge case.

- If the recheck is still off, refine the same local region before opening a distant new theory.
- Once the failing path and its directly coupled region look consistent, finalize the code.

Try to avoid patterns like these:
- staying in diagnosis mode for too long without testing the next plausible edit;

```
class Solution:
    def findAllRecipes(self, recepies: List[str], ingredients: List[List[str]], supplies: List[str]) -> List[str]:
        dct=defaultdict(lambda :[])
        indegree={}
        n=len(recepies)

        for i in recepies:
            indegree[i]=0

        for i in range(n+1)   
            for j in ingredients[i]:
                indegree[j]=0

        for i in range(n):
            for j in ingredients[i]:
                dct[j].append(recepies[i])
                indegree[recepies[i]]+=1

        st=[]
        for i in indegree:
            if indegree[i]==0:
                st.push(i)
        flst=[]
        ans=defaultdict(lambda :[])
        while st:
            x=st.pop(0)
            for i in dct[x]:
                for j in ans[x]:
                    if j not in ans[i]:
                        ans[i].append(j)
                ans[i].append(x)
                indegree[i]-=1
                if indegree[i]==0:
                    st.append(i)
            if x in recepies:
                for k in ans[x]:
                    if k not in supplies:
                        break
                else:
                    flst.append(x)
                    supplies.append(x)

        return iflst
```

Now, let's decide final code. We can write:

```
from typing import List
from collections import defaultdict

class Solution:
    def findAllRecipes(self, recipes: List[str], ingredients: List[List[str]], supplies: List[str]) -> List[str]:
        dct = defaultdict(list)
        indegree = {}
        n = len(recipes)

        for recipe in recipes:
            indegree[recipe] = 0

        for ing_list in ingredients:
            for ing in ing_list:
                indegree[ing] = 0

        for i in range(n):
            for ing in ingredients[i]:
                dct[ing].append(recipes[i])
                indegree[recipes[i]] += 1

        st = []
        for node in indegree:
            if indegree[node] == 0:
                st.append(node)

        flst = []
        ans = defaultdict(list)

        while st:
            x = st.pop(0)
            for recipe in dct[x]:
                for pre in ans[x]:
                    if pre not in ans[recipe]:
                        ans[recipe].append(pre)
                ans[recipe].append(x)
                indegree[recipe] -= 1
                if indegree[recipe] == 0:
                    st.append(recipe)

            if x in recipes:
                for pre in ans[x]:
                    if pre not in supplies:
                        break
                else:
                    flst.append(x)
                    supplies.append(x)

        return flst
```

Need check if `x in recipes` is O(n) and if `recipes` is list. Fine.

But wait: In the second loop, I changed `for i in range(n+1)` to `for ing_list in ingredients`. This sets indegree for all ingredients. But if an ingredient is a recipe, it resets indegree to 0. Good. But what if an ingredient is a recipe and also has been initialized to 0, okay. However, if an ingredient is a recipe and appears in `ingredients` after its indegree has been incremented? No, this loop runs before edge building, so no increments yet. Good.

But there is a subtle difference: The original second loop was `for i in range(n+1)` but should be `range(n)`. My `for ing_list in ingredients` is equivalent to `range(n)`. Good.

Now, one issue: In the original code, the second loop sets `indegree[j]=0` for all ingredients. But if an ingredient is a recipe, and that recipe has already been initialized to 0, okay. But if an ingredient is a recipe and also has its own ingredients, its indegree will be set to 0 in second loop, then incremented in edge-building loop. Good.

Now, what about supplies that are not ingredients? They are not in indegree. But they don't need to be processed. If a supply is not used, no effect. If a supply is used, it's in indegree. Good.

Wait indegree A=1, B=0. st [B]. Pop B: for recipe A, ans[A].append(B), indegree A=0, st.append(A). Check B: B in recipes, ans[B]=[] -> add B, supplies [B]. </think>

=== RAW ANSWER ===
```python
from typing import List
from collections import defaultdict

class Solution:
    def findAllRecipes(self, recipes: List[str], ingredients: List[List[str]], supplies: List[str]) -> List[str]:
        dct = defaultdict(list)
        indegree = {}
        n = len(recipes)

        for recipe in recipes:
            indegree[recipe] = 0

        for ing_list in ingredients:
            for ing in ing_list:
                indegree[ing] = 0

        for i in range(n):
            if recipes[i] in supplies:
                continue
            for ing in ingredients[i]:
                dct[ing].append(recipes[i])
                indegree[recipes[i]] += 1

        st = []
        for node in indegree:
            if indegree[node] == 0:
                st.append(node)

        flst = []
        ans = defaultdict(list)

        while st:
            x = st.pop(0)

            for recipe in dct[x]:
                for pre in ans[x]:
                    if pre not in ans[recipe]:
                        ans[recipe].append(pre)
                ans[recipe].append(x)
                indegree[recipe] -= 1
                if indegree[recipe] == 0:
                    st.append(recipe)

            if x in recipes:
                for pre in ans[x]:
                    if pre not in supplies:
                        break
                else:
                    flst.append(x)
                    if x not in supplies:
                        supplies.append(x)

        return flst
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
from typing import List
from collections import defaultdict

class Solution:
    def findAllRecipes(self, recipes: List[str], ingredients: List[List[str]], supplies: List[str]) -> List[str]:
        dct = defaultdict(list)
        indegree = {}
        n = len(recipes)

        for recipe in recipes:
            indegree[recipe] = 0

        for ing_list in ingredients:
            for ing in ing_list:
                indegree[ing] = 0

        for i in range(n):
            if recipes[i] in supplies:
                continue
            for ing in ingredients[i]:
                dct[ing].append(recipes[i])
                indegree[recipes[i]] += 1

        st = []
        for node in indegree:
            if indegree[node] == 0:
                st.append(node)

        flst = []
        ans = defaultdict(list)

        while st:
            x = st.pop(0)

            for recipe in dct[x]:
                for pre in ans[x]:
                    if pre not in ans[recipe]:
                        ans[recipe].append(pre)
                ans[recipe].append(x)
                indegree[recipe] -= 1
                if indegree[recipe] == 0:
                    st.append(recipe)

            if x in recipes:
                for pre in ans[x]:
                    if pre not in supplies:
                        break
                else:
                    flst.append(x)
                    if x not in supplies:
                        supplies.append(x)

        return flst
